The totals are 4 (1+3) and 6 (2+4). But it might also use render_rows to render the total row. However, render_rows currently doesn't know about totals marking. It might just render a row as comma-separated values. For the total row, we want \"TOTAL,4,6\". That's not just the totals values; it has the word \"TOTAL\" prefix. So render_rows probably shouldn't be used for that row; instead, export_csv should manually format the total row as \"TOTAL,\" + \",\".join(map(str, totals)).\n\n   Alternatively, we could modify render_rows to accept an optional prefix parameter, but that's more changes.\n\n   Given the simplicity, we can adjust export_csv to:\n   - If totals is True, compute totals = column_totals(rows)\n   - Then get the regular rows rendered: \"\\n\".join(render_rows(rows))\n   - Then append \"\\nTOTAL,\" + \",\".join(map(str, totals))\n   - But note that column_totals now needs to handle non-numeric values (from the first test).
